\\mathrm{F}}{\\partial \\mathrm{x}} = \\frac {\\partial \\left(\\mathrm{x} ^ {2} - 6 \\mathrm{xy} + 1 0 \\mathrm{y} ^ {2} - 2 \\mathrm{yz} - \\mathrm{z} ^ {2} + 1 8\\right)}{\\partial \\mathrm{x}} \\\\ \\quad = \\frac {\\mathrm{d} \\left(\\mathrm{x} ^ {2}\\right)}{\\mathrm{dx}} - \\frac {\\partial (6 \\mathrm{xy})}{\\partial \\mathrm{x}} + \\frac {\\partial (1 0 \\mathrm{y} ^ {2})}{\\partial = 2 \\mathrm{x} - 6 \\mathrm{y} + 0 - 0 - 0 + 0 \\\\ \\quad = 2 \\mathrm{x} - 6 \\mathrm{y} \\\\ \\frac {\\partial \\mathrm{F}}{\\partial \\mathrm{y}} = \\frac {\\partial \\left(\\mathrm{x} ^ {2} - 6 \\mathrm{xy} + 1 0 \\mathrm{y} ^ {2} - 2 \\mathrm{yz} - \\mathrm{z} ^ {2} + 1 8\\right)}{\\partial \\mathrm{y}} \\\\ \\quad = \\frac {\\partial (\\mathrm{x} ^ {2})}{\\partial \\mathrm{y}} - \\frac {\\partial (6 \\mathrm{xy})}{\\partial \\mathrm{y}} + \\frac {\\mathrm{d} (1 0 \\mathrm{y} ^ {2})}{\\mathrm{dy}} - \\frac {\\partial (2 \\mathrm{yz})}{\\partial \\mathrm{y}} - \\frac {\\partial y}}{\\frac {\\partial F}{\\partial z}} \\\\ = - \\frac {2 x - 6 y}{- 2 y - 2 z} & & = - \\frac {- 6 x + 2 0 y - 2 z}{- 2 y - 2 z} \\\\ = \\frac {x - 3 y}{y + z} & & = \\frac {- 3 x + 1 0 y - z}{y + z} 3)} = \\frac {5}{3}} \\\\ & & {\\mathrm{A} = \\frac {\\partial^ {2} \\mathrm{z}}{\\partial \\mathrm{x} ^ {2}} \\Big | _ {(- 9, - 3, - 3)} = - \\frac {1}{6}} & & {\\mathrm{B} = \\frac {\\partial^ {2} = - 3\n$$\n\n## 利用定义判断极值点\n\n例1 . 求函数 $\\scriptstyle \\mathbf { z } = \\mathbf { x } ^ { 2 } \\mathbf { y } + \\mathbf { x } \\mathbf { y } ^ { 2 }$ 的极值\n\n$$\n① \\frac {\\partial \\mathrm{z}}{\\partial \\mathrm{x}} = \\frac {\\partial (\\mathrm{x} ^ {2} \\mathrm{y} + \\mathrm{xy} ^ {2})}{\\partial \\mathrm{x}} = 2 \\mathrm{xy} + \\mathrm{y} ^ {2}\n$$\n\n$$\n\\frac {\\partial \\mathrm{z}}{\\partial \\mathrm{y}} = \\frac {\\partial (\\mathrm{x} ^ {2} \\mathrm{y} + \\mathrm{xy} ^ {2})}{\\partial \\mathrm{y}} = \\mathrm{x} ^ {2} + 2 \\mathrm{xy}\n$$\n\n$$\n\\text {②} \\left\\{ \\begin{array}{l} \\frac {\\partial z}{\\partial x} = 0 \\\\ \\frac {\\partial z}{\\partial y} = 0 \\end{array} \\Rightarrow \\left\\{ \\begin{array}{l l} 2 x y + y ^ {2} = 0 & (1) \\\\ x ^ {2} + 2 x y = 0 & (2) \\end{array} \\right. \\right.\n$$\n\n$$\n\\stackrel {(1) - (2) \\text {得}} {=} \\mathrm{y} ^ {2} - \\mathrm{x} ^ {2} = 0 \\Rightarrow \\mathrm{x} = \\pm \\mathrm{y} \\tag {3}\n$$\n\n3 带入 2 得 $y ^ { 2 } \\pm 2 y ^ { 2 } = 0 \\implies \\mathrm { y } = 0 \\ ( 4 )$\n\n4 带入 3 得 $\\left\\{ { \\begin{array} { l } { \\mathbf { y } = 0 } \\\\ { \\mathbf { x } = 0 } \\end{array} } \\right.$ ∴ 满足 $\\left\\{ { \\begin{array} { l } { { \\frac { \\partial \\mathrm { z } } { \\partial \\mathrm { x } } } = 0 } \\\\ { { \\frac { \\partial \\mathrm { z } } { \\partial \\mathrm { y } } } = 0 } \\end{array} } \\right.$ 的一组解：(0,0)\n\n③ $\\begin{array} { r } { \\mathrm { A } = \\frac { \\partial ^ { 2 } \\mathbf { z } } { \\partial \\mathbf { x } ^ { 2 } } \\Big | _ { ( 0 , 0 ) } } & { = 0 \\qquad \\mathrm { B } = \\frac { \\partial ^ { 2 } \\mathbf { z } } { \\partial \\mathbf { x } \\partial \\mathbf { y } } \\Big | _ { ( 0 , 0 ) } = 0 \\qquad \\mathrm { C } = \\frac { \\partial ^ { 2 } \\mathbf { z } } { \\partial \\mathbf { y } ^ { 2 } } \\Big | _ { ( 0 , 0 ) } = 0 } \\end{array}$\n\n④ 解为 (0,0) 时：\n\n$$\n\\begin{array}{c} \\mathrm{B} ^ {2} - \\mathrm{AC} = 0 ^ {2} - 0 \\cdot 0 \\\\ = 0 \\end{array}\n$$\n\n∴ 不确定(0,0) 是不是极值点\n\n$$\n\\mathrm{f} (0, 0) = 0 ^ {2} \\cdot 0 + 0 \\cdot 0 ^ {2} = 0\n$$\n\n$$\n\\lim_{\\substack{\\mathrm{x}\\to 0\\\\ \\mathrm{y}\\to 0}}\\mathrm{f}(\\mathrm{x},\\mathrm{y}) = \\lim_{\\substack{\\mathrm{x}\\to 0\\\\ \\mathrm{y}\\to 0}}(\\mathrm{x}^{2}\\mathrm{y} + \\mathrm{xy}^{2}) = 0^{2}\\cdot 0 + 0\\cdot 0^{2} = 0\n$$\n\n$$\n\\lim_{\\substack{\\mathrm{x}\\to 0\\\\ \\mathrm{y}\\to 0}}\\mathrm{f}(\\mathrm{x},\\mathrm{y}) = \\mathrm{f}(0,0)\n$$\n\n$$\n\\lim_{\\substack{x\\to 0^{+}\\\\ y\\to 0^{+}}}f(x,y) = y\\to 0}}f(x,y)\n$$\n\n分析(0,0)周围的值是不是\n\n$$\n\\left\\{ \\begin{array}{l} \\lim _ {\\substack {\\mathrm{x} \\to 0 ^ {+} \\\\ \\mathrm{y} \\to 0 ^ {+}}} \\mathrm{f} (\\mathrm{x}, \\mathrm{y}) 0}}f(x,y)\n$$\n\n$$\n\\lim_{\\substack{\\mathrm{x}\\to 0\\\\ \\mathrm{y}\\to 0}}\\frac{\\mathrm{f}(\\mathrm{x},\\mathrm{y})}{\\sin(\\mathrm{x}^{2} + \\mathrm{y}^{2})} <  0\n$$\n\n∴ $\\operatorname* { \\\\ \\lim _ {\\substack {x \\to 0 ^ {+} \\\\ y \\to 0 ^ {-}}} f (x, y) \\\\ \\lim _ {\\substack {x \\to 0 ^ {-} \\\\ y \\to 0 ^ {+}}} f (x, y) \\\\ \\lim _ {\\substack {x \\to 0 ^ {-} \\\\ y \\to 0 ^ {-}}} f (x, y) \\end{array} \\right.\n$$\n\n或者\n\n都比f(0,0)小 极大值\n\n或者\n\n有的比f(0,0)大，有的比\n\nf(0,0)小 不是极值\n\n$$\n\\lim_{\\substack{x\\to 0\\\\ y\\to 0}}f(x,y)\\qquad \\lim_{\\substack{x\\to 0^{+}\\\\ 0^{-}\\\\ y\\to 0^{-}}}\\mathrm{f}(x,y)\n$$\n\n$$\n\\boxed { \\begin{array}{l l} \\lim _ {\\substack {x \\to 0 \\\\ y \\to 0}} \\frac {f (x , y) - x y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {2}} = 1 & \\frac {f (x , {的式子} = A \\\\ \\text {则} \\text {带} f (x, y) \\text {的式子} = A + \\alpha , \\lim \\alpha = 0 \\end{array}\n$$\n\n$$\n\\begin{array}{r l} \\frac {\\mathrm{f(x,y)-xy}}{(\\mathrm{x} ^ {2} + \\mathrm{y} ^ {4}) <   f (0, 0) \\\\ \\lim _ {\\substack {x \\to 0 ^ {-} \\\\ y \\to 0 ^ {+}}} f (x, y) = \\lim _ {\\substack {x \\to 0 ^ {-} \\\\ y \\to 0 ^ {+}}} [ x y + (1 + 0) (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {2} ] =
